260b2(ja): 'jug ma la 'gro ma//gal te zhag ni dgun yang /sa ni g.yes par gyur pa na/sa la ci dgar spyod par nus/sa ni g.yes pa'i sgra ldan ni//sang+yas 'bras bu mi snang ste//de bzhinas dang lam rnams la/yun du sbyang zhing gnas pa'o//yang nyon rngo mkha 'gro ma//nga yis bstan pa
260b3(ja): 'di ltar son tshad kyi//tshe'i 'du byed byed pa'i tshe//na mkha' yi ni mtshan nyid kyis//'di kun skye ba'i gnas la spyod//gang zhig shi ba de tshe na//namakha' chu gnag mun pa gnag/de bzhin char dang khu tshangs dang //rlung dang ser bu bu yug dang //'di kun su la byung ba de//dmyal ba yo
260b4(ja): ni skye gnasu 'di la bar do med pa la//'gyur ba yini mkha 'gro ma//na mkha' na bun lha bu la//'od kyi zer la nyi zla ni//rlung dang ser bu med pa la//ser klog nyi ma mi snang dang //char bab shul la yang na ni//de'i srod la 'bab par 'gyur//'di ltar de bzhin 'byung ba de//yi dgas kyi ni
260b5(ja): skye gnas//skye ba yi no mkha 'gro ma//na mkha' rmugs shing rlung yang med//spri ni gnag la go mtshams med//yang ni pan bun snang ba dang //rmugs shing nyams mi dga' ba la/tshe'i 'du byed dus byas pa//'di ni byol song gnasu'o//shin tu drag cing rlung gis 'khrugs//sprin na
260b6(ja): kham nag 'jigs pa'i gzugs//rab tu 'khrug cing 'gro ba bcas//'brug dang klog gi skad 'byin dang //nyi ma zla ba snang mi nus//'di ltar su la byung ba de//'di ni lha min skye gnaso//na mkha' gsal zhing dngas pa la//nyi ma zla ba'i snang ba gsal//rlung dang ser bu snang mi nus/'ad
260b7(ja): mab+ha'i skye gnaso//yang nam pa 'gas la dngas la/mi 'byed sprin ni dar dkar bzhin/shin h+ya srab cing rnam par snang //yang na nyi bla 'od khyim can//'di ltar su la byung ba de/mi yi s+ye gnas 'dis thob bo//'di ltar rtags dang su ldan pa//nges par rigs drug nges pa yin/'di ltar
